Volume - 160 BENGALURU, SATURDAY 15, NOVEMBER, 2025 (KARTHIKA, 24, SHAKAVARSHA, 1947) Issue 216

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
No.:RC-CFS0ADMN/315/2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat
E-1868221 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560001
Date:10-11-2025

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Gandhi Nagar village of
Sindhanur Taluk Raichur District and to constitute two new revenue villages
called Tayamma camp and Venkateshwra camp as specified in schedules along
with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Raichur District.
(11029)

QNCAO BMF UE TBD, BAW OB, Shower, 90.928 on YA

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alterthe limits of Gorebal and Sasalamari Village of
Sindhanur taluk Raichur District and to constitute a new revenue villages
called Gourinagar, Mudalagirinagar, Sriramnagar as specified in schedule
along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Raichur District.

PR-1503
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
No.:RC-CFS/303/2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1942273 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, 560001
Date: 06-11-2025

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Bilidale Village of Kanakapura
Taluk of Bangalore South District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Chamundipura as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Bilidale village for conversion of Chamundipura unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Bangalore
South District.
SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the table
shall be declared ashabitation area of Chamundipura village of Kanakapura Taluk of
Bangalore South District convert the same to form hamlet within the boundary of Bilidale
Revenue village and same will herein after be named as called Chamundipura of Bilidale
village in Kanakapura Taluk of Bangalore South District.

PR-1532

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RC-CFS/255/2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1940818 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, 560001
Date:12-11-2025.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
Specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience
the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Hanumantapura Village of Jagalur Taluk of Davanagere District and to constitute a
new revenue subvillage called Amruthapura as specified in schedule.

Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Hanumantapura village for conversion Amruthapura unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/part of the said revenue village.
Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be
affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after
thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date Specified above
will be considered by the state Government. Objection or suggestion may be
addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Davanagere District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Amruthapura village of Jagalur Taluk of
Davanagere District convert the same to form hamlet within the boundary of
Hanumantapura Revenue village and same will herein after be named as called
Amruthapura of Hanumantapura village in Jagalur Taluk of Davanagere District.

Jagalur Taluk of Davanagere District.

The proposal specified above shall come into effect from such date as the State

Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose s
to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Keelara Village of Kunigal Taluk of
Tumakuru District and to constitute a new revenue villages called Keelara
Janatha Badavane as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Keelara village for conversion of Keelara Janatha Badavane unrecorded habitation
in to hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.

District.

District.

PR – 1582

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

No.:RC-CFS/206/2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1940540 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, 560001
Date:12-11-2025

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Mallapura Village of Jagalur Taluk
of Davanagere District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Mallapura Hosuru as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Mallapura village for conversion of Mallapura Hosuru unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Davanagere District.

SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared ashabitation area of Mallapura Hosuru village of Jagalur
Taluk of Davanagere District convert the same to form hamlet within the boundary
of Mallapura Revenue village and same will herein after be named as called
Mallapura Hosuru of Mallapura village in Jagalur Taluk of Davanagere District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by section5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964(Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (1) to (4) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population ,area of the village , the demand under land revenue in the
village and having regard to the administrative convenience the Government of
Karnataka propose to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Venkatapur village of
Maski Taluk, Raichur District and to constitute a new village called Maruti Nagar
as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new survey
numbers assigned to them.
Now therefore in Exercise of the power conferred by section 6of the said act
notice is is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken in to consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Raichur District.

SCHEDULE - 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Venkatapur villages of Maski Taluk in Raichur District to form a new revenue
village called as Maruti Nagar of Maski Taluk and Raichur district along with
the survey numbers includedin column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

No.:RC-CFS0ADMN/142/2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat
E-1827632 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date: 10-11-2025.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Hiredinni Village of Maski
Taluk, Raichur District and to constitute a new revenue villages called Sri
Ramanagar as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers
and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date

Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Raichur District.

SCHEDULE - 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Hiredinni villages of Maski Taluk in Raichur District to form a new revenue
village called as Sri Ramanagar of Maski Taluk and Raichur district along with
the survey numbers includedin column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Somalapur village of
Sindhanur Taluk Raichur District and to constitute a new revenue villages
called Venkatapur as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey
numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Raichur District.

Somalapur village of Sindhanur taluk in Raichur District to form a new revenue
village called as Venkatapur of Sindhanur taluk Raichur District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR – 1589

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RC-CFS/207/2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat
E - 1940541 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date: 11-11-2025

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the, Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Sagarapete Village of
Channagiri taluk Davanagere District and to constitute a new revenue
subvillage called Sagarapete Camp as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Sagarapete village for conversion of Sagarapete Camp unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Davanagere District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Sagarapete Camp village of
Channagiri Taluk of Davanagere District convert the same to form hamlet within the
boundary of Sagarapete Revenue village and same will here in after be named as
called Sagarapete Camp of Sagarapete village in Channagiri Taluk of
Davanagere District.

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka proposes
to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Sasalahalli Village of Tiptur Taluk of
Tumakuru District and to constitute a new revenue village called Sasalahalli
Badavane as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Sasalahalli village for conversion of Sasalahalli Badavane unrecorded habitation in
to hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Tumakuru
District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka proposes
to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Harachanahalli Village of Tiptur Taluk
of Tumakuru District and to constitute a new revenue village called
Harachanahalli Badavane as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Harachanahalli village for conversion of Harachanahalli Badavane unrecorded
habitation in to hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Tumakuru District.

Revenue Act 1964(Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses (1) to (4) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules

as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new survey
numbers assigned to them.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
No.:RC-CFS0ADMN/284/2025 Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1862928 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, 560001
Date: 04-10-2025

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand

under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrativeconvenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alterthe limits of Shivoor Village of Afzalpur Taluk of
Kalaburagi District and to constitute a new revenue villages called Shivoor
Hosanagar as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Shivoor village for conversion of Shivoor Hosanagar unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Kalaburagi District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing surveynumbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Shivoor Hosanagar village of Afzalpur
Taluk of Kalaburagi District convert the same to form hamlet within the boundary of
Shivoor Revenue village and same will here in after be named as called Shivoor
Hosanagar of Shivoor village in Afzalpur Taluk of Kalaburagi District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such
as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience
the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
S.Ramaladinni and Kabberal.D Village of Maski Taluk, Raichur District and to constitute
a new revenue villages called Shankara Nagara as specified in schedule along with
maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act, notice
is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected thereby that
the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or suggestion
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Raichur District.

SCHEDULE-I
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas specified in
column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said S.Ramaladinni
and Kabberal.D villages of Maski Taluka Raichur District to from a new revenue village
called as Shankara Nagara of Maski Taluka and Raichur District along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.
